## 68. LaunchContext::SetLaunchMode

完整范围：[launch_context.cc:L167–L243](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/base_comm/primitives/launch_context.cc#L167-L243)；文件：`hcomm/src/base_comm/primitives/launch_context.cc`。

- [S139 / L176](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/base_comm/primitives/launch_context.cc#L176)：设置当前提交标签为/按`defaultTag ? "" : std::string(launchTag)`（是否未指定提交标签、任务提交标签）；调用std::string，使用是否未指定提交标签、任务提交标签。

- [S47 / L51](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/connection/aicpu/ub_conn_lite.cc#L51)：设置u32 cqeEn为/按`(cfg.cqeEn && (slicePos == SlicePosition::LAST || slicePos == SlicePosition::ONLY)) ? 1 : 0`（UB WQE保序/完成配置的cqeEn字段、当前分片首/中/尾位置）。

## 83. UbConnLite::ProcessSlices

按最大 sliceSize 切分整片和尾片，标记 FIRST/MIDDLE/LAST/ONLY

完整范围：[ub_conn_lite.cc:L181–L272](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/connection/aicpu/ub_conn_lite.cc#L181-L272)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/connection/aicpu/ub_conn_lite.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S133 / L207](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/connection/aicpu/ub_conn_lite.cc#L207)：仅当`(UNLIKELY(loc.GetAddr() > UINT64_MAX - totalSize || rmt.GetAddr() > UINT64_MAX - totalSize))`（本端缓冲区/地址的GetAddr字段、累计传输字节数、远端缓冲区/地址的GetAddr字段）成立时进入此分支；读取缓冲区起始地址。

- [S136 / L213](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/connection/aicpu/ub_conn_lite.cc#L213)：按`(u64 sliceIdx = 0; sliceIdx < sliceNum; sliceIdx++)`（当前完整分片下标、完整分片条数）遍历本批条目/分片；各次处理保持数组对应关系。

- [S148 / L234](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/connection/aicpu/ub_conn_lite.cc#L234)：设置当前分片首/中/尾位置为/按`(sliceIdx == 0) ? SlicePosition::FIRST : SlicePosition::MIDDLE`（当前完整分片下标）。

完整范围：[rtsq_a5.cc:L60–L143](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L60-L143)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S63 / L68](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L68)：编译条件`ifdef CCL_KERNEL_AICPU`限定后续实现，区分Host/设备或构建能力分支。

- [S65 / L72](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L72)：结束前述编译条件控制的实现片段。

- [S72 / L85](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L85)：在`(availableSpace <= pendingSqeCnt)`（RTSQ可用槽位数、本地待提交SQE条数）条件下重复执行后续等待或分片处理。

- [S75 / L91](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L91)：仅当`(availableSpace > pendingSqeCnt)`（RTSQ可用槽位数、本地待提交SQE条数）成立时进入此分支。

- [S80 / L100](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L100)：仅当`(UNLIKELY(curTime - lastPrintTime >= printInterval))`（当前单调时钟时刻、上次状态打印时刻、状态日志打印间隔）成立时进入此分支。

- [S89 / L117](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L117)：编译条件`ifdef CCL_KERNEL_AICPU`限定后续实现，区分Host/设备或构建能力分支。

- [S91 / L121](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L121)：仅当`(UNLIKELY(ret != HCCL_SUCCESS))`（当前调用状态）成立时进入此分支。

- [S97 / L133](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L133)：结束前述编译条件控制的实现片段。

- [S98 / L135](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L135)：仅当`(checkOpExecStatusCallback_ != nullptr)`成立时进入此分支。

## 92. RtsqA5::CopySqeBufToSq

按环队列是否回绕一次或两次复制 SQE 缓存到 RTSQ VA

完整范围：[rtsq_a5.cc:L170–L256](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L170-L256)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S131 / L176](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L176)：仅当`(sqTail_ >= sqHead_)`（软件保存的RTSQ尾槽位、软件保存的RTSQ头槽位）成立时进入此分支。

- [S133 / L180](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L180)：仅当`(pendingSqeCnt <= depthLeft)`（本地待提交SQE条数、RTSQ尾部到数组末尾的剩余槽数）成立时进入此分支。

- [S139 / L192](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L192)：仅当`(UNLIKELY(ret != 0))`（当前调用状态）成立时进入此分支。

- [S142 / L198](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L198)：进入上一个条件未命中的替代路径，按本函数的资源/设备/协议分流继续处理。

- [S149 / L211](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L211)：仅当`(ret != 0)`（当前调用状态）成立时进入此分支。

- [S157 / L226](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L226)：仅当`(UNLIKELY(ret != 0))`（当前调用状态）成立时进入此分支。

## 93. RtsqA5::LaunchTask

确保空间并复制本地 SQE 到设备 RTSQ，更新硬件 SQ 尾触发执行，按需保存任务缓存

完整范围：[rtsq_a5.cc:L288–L379](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L288-L379)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S206 / L294](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L294)：仅当`(pendingSqeCnt == 0)`（本地待提交SQE条数）成立时进入此分支。

- [S213 / L306](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L306)：仅当`(pendingSqeCnt == 0)`（本地待提交SQE条数）成立时进入此分支。

- [S223 / L322](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L322)：仅当`((UNLIKELY(GetPlfDebugConfigValue() & PLF_TASK)) || UNLIKELY(HcclCheckLogLevel(HCCL_LOG_INFO)))`成立时进入此分支；调用GetPlfDebugConfigValue, HcclCheckLogLevel。

- [S229 / L333](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L333)：按`(size_t sqeIdx = 0; sqeIdx < pendingSqeCnt; sqeIdx++)`（当前SQE下标、本地待提交SQE条数）遍历本批条目/分片；各次处理保持数组对应关系。

- [S232 / L339](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L339)：仅当`(UNLIKELY(ret != HCCL_SUCCESS))`（当前调用状态）成立时进入此分支。

- [S246 / L362](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L362)：仅当`(needCacheTask)`（是否记录本轮任务缓存）成立时进入此分支。
